Appendix A. Evaluation Against Tunnel-Based EAP Method Requirements

Appendix A. トンネルベースの EAP 方式の要件に照らした評価

This section evaluates all tunnel-based EAP method requirements described in [RFC6678] against TEAP version 1.

この節では、[RFC6678] で説明されているトンネルベースの EAP 方式のすべての要件に照らして、TEAP バージョン 1 を評価します。

A.1. Requirement 4.1.1: RFC Compliance

A.1. 要件 4.1.1: RFC への準拠

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by being compliant with [RFC3748], [RFC4017], [RFC5247], and [RFC4962]. It is also compliant with the "cryptographic algorithm agility" requirement by leveraging TLS 1.2 for all cryptographic algorithm negotiation.

TEAPv1 は、[RFC3748]、[RFC4017]、[RFC5247]、[RFC4962] に準拠することで、この要件を満たしています。また、すべての暗号アルゴリズムの交渉に TLS 1.2 を活用することで、「暗号アルゴリズムの俊敏性」の要件にも準拠しています。

A.2. Requirement 4.2.1: TLS Requirements

A.2. 要件 4.2.1: TLS の要件

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by mandating TLS version 1.2 support as defined in Section 3.2.

TEAPv1 は、Section 3.2 で定義されているように TLS バージョン 1.2 のサポートを義務付けることで、この要件を満たしています。

A.3. Requirement 4.2.1.1.1: Cipher Suite Negotiation

A.3. 要件 4.2.1.1.1: 暗号スイートの交渉

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by using TLS to provide protected cipher suite negotiation.

TEAPv1 は、TLS を使用して保護された暗号スイートの交渉を提供することで、この要件を満たしています。

A.4. Requirement 4.2.1.1.2: Tunnel Data Protection Algorithms

A.4. 要件 4.2.1.1.2: トンネルのデータ保護アルゴリズム

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by mandating cipher suites as defined in Section 3.2.

TEAPv1 は、Section 3.2 で定義されているように暗号スイートを義務付けることで、この要件を満たしています。

A.5. Requirement 4.2.1.1.3: Tunnel Authentication and Key Establishment

A.5. 要件 4.2.1.1.3: トンネルの認証と鍵の確立

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by mandating cipher suites that only include cipher suites that use strong cryptographic algorithms. They do not include cipher suites providing mutually anonymous authentication or static Diffie-Hellman cipher suites as defined in Section 3.2.

TEAPv1 は、強力な暗号アルゴリズムを使用する暗号スイートだけを含む暗号スイートを義務付けることで、この要件を満たしています。それらには、Section 3.2 で定義されている、相互に匿名の認証を提供する暗号スイートや静的な Diffie-Hellman の暗号スイートは含まれません。

A.6. Requirement 4.2.1.2: Tunnel Replay Protection

A.6. 要件 4.2.1.2: トンネルのリプレイ保護

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by using TLS to provide sufficient replay protection.

TEAPv1 は、TLS を使用して十分なリプレイ保護を提供することで、この要件を満たしています。

A.7. Requirement 4.2.1.3: TLS Extensions

A.7. 要件 4.2.1.3: TLS 拡張

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by allowing TLS extensions, such as TLS Certificate Status Request extension [RFC6066] and SessionTicket extension [RFC5077], to be used during TLS tunnel establishment.

TEAPv1 は、TLS トンネルの確立の間に、TLS Certificate Status Request 拡張 [RFC6066] や SessionTicket 拡張 [RFC5077] などの TLS 拡張の使用を許可することで、この要件を満たしています。

A.8. Requirement 4.2.1.4: Peer Identity Privacy

A.8. 要件 4.2.1.4: ピアのアイデンティティーのプライバシー

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by establishment of the TLS tunnel and protection identities specific to the Inner Method. In addition, the peer certificate can be sent confidentially (i.e., encrypted).

TEAPv1 は、TLS トンネルの確立と、内部方式に固有のアイデンティティーの保護によって、この要件を満たしています。さらに、ピアの証明書を機密に（つまり暗号化して）送信できます。

A.9. Requirement 4.2.1.5: Session Resumption

A.9. 要件 4.2.1.5: セッションの再開

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by mandating support of TLS session resumption as defined in Section 3.5.1 and TLS session resumption using the methods defined in [RFC9190].

TEAPv1 は、Section 3.5.1 で定義されている TLS セッションの再開と、[RFC9190] で定義されている方法を使用した TLS セッションの再開のサポートを義務付けることで、この要件を満たしています。

A.10. Requirement 4.2.2: Fragmentation

A.10. 要件 4.2.2: 断片化

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by leveraging fragmentation support provided by TLS as defined in Section 3.10.

TEAPv1 は、Section 3.10 で定義されているように TLS が提供する断片化のサポートを活用することで、この要件を満たしています。

A.11. Requirement 4.2.3: Protection of Data External to Tunnel

A.11. 要件 4.2.3: トンネルの外部のデータの保護

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by including the TEAP version number received in the computation of the Crypto-Binding TLV as defined in Section 4.2.13.

TEAPv1 は、Section 4.2.13 で定義されているように、受信した TEAP のバージョン番号を Crypto-Binding TLV の計算に含めることで、この要件を満たしています。

A.12. Requirement 4.3.1: Extensible Attribute Types

A.12. 要件 4.3.1: 拡張可能な属性タイプ

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by using an extensible TLV data layer inside the tunnel as defined in Section 4.2.

TEAPv1 は、Section 4.2 で定義されているように、トンネル内で拡張可能な TLV のデータ層を使用することで、この要件を満たしています。

A.13. Requirement 4.3.2: Request/Challenge Response Operation

A.13. 要件 4.3.2: 要求とチャレンジ応答の動作

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by allowing multiple TLVs to be sent in a single EAP request or response packet, while maintaining the half-duplex operation typical of EAP.

TEAPv1 は、EAP に典型的な半二重の動作を維持しながら、単一の EAP の要求または応答のパケットで複数の TLV を送信できるようにすることで、この要件を満たしています。

A.14. Requirement 4.3.3: Indicating Criticality of Attributes

A.14. 要件 4.3.3: 属性の重要度の表示

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by having a mandatory bit in each TLV to indicate whether it is mandatory to support or not as defined in Section 4.2.

TEAPv1 は、Section 4.2 で定義されているように、サポートが必須かどうかを示す必須ビットを各 TLV に持たせることで、この要件を満たしています。

A.15. Requirement 4.3.4: Vendor-Specific Support

A.15. 要件 4.3.4: ベンダー固有のサポート

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by having a Vendor-Specific TLV to allow vendors to define their own attributes as defined in Section 4.2.8.

TEAPv1 は、Section 4.2.8 で定義されているように、ベンダーが独自の属性を定義できるようにする Vendor-Specific TLV を持つことで、この要件を満たしています。

A.16. Requirement 4.3.5: Result Indication

A.16. 要件 4.3.5: 結果の表示

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by having a Result TLV to exchange the final result of the TEAP authentication so both the peer and server have a synchronized state as defined in Section 4.2.4.

TEAPv1 は、Section 4.2.4 で定義されているように、ピアとサーバーの両方が同期した状態を持つように TEAP の認証の最終結果を交換するための Result TLV を持つことで、この要件を満たしています。

A.17. Requirement 4.3.6: Internationalization of Display Strings

A.17. 要件 4.3.6: 表示文字列の国際化

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by supporting UTF-8 format in the Basic-Password-Auth-Req TLV as defined in Section 4.2.14 and the Basic-Password-Auth-Resp TLV as defined in Section 4.2.15.

TEAPv1 は、Section 4.2.14 で定義されている Basic-Password-Auth-Req TLV と Section 4.2.15 で定義されている Basic-Password-Auth-Resp TLV で UTF-8 形式をサポートすることで、この要件を満たしています。

A.18. Requirement 4.4: EAP Channel-Binding Requirements

A.18. 要件 4.4: EAP のチャネルバインディングの要件

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by having a Channel-Binding TLV to exchange the EAP channel-binding data as defined in Section 4.2.7.

TEAPv1 は、Section 4.2.7 で定義されているように、EAP のチャネルバインディングのデータを交換するための Channel-Binding TLV を持つことで、この要件を満たしています。

A.19. Requirement 4.5.1.1: Confidentiality and Integrity

A.19. 要件 4.5.1.1: 機密性と完全性

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by running the password authentication inside a protected TLS tunnel.

TEAPv1 は、保護された TLS トンネルの内部でパスワード認証を実行することで、この要件を満たしています。

A.20. Requirement 4.5.1.2: Authentication of Server

A.20. 要件 4.5.1.2: サーバーの認証

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by mandating authentication of the server before establishment of the protected TLS and then running inner password authentication as defined in Section 3.2.

TEAPv1 は、Section 3.2 で定義されているように、保護された TLS を確立する前にサーバーの認証を義務付け、その後で内部のパスワード認証を実行することで、この要件を満たしています。

A.21. Requirement 4.5.1.3: Server Certificate Revocation Checking

A.21. 要件 4.5.1.3: サーバー証明書の失効チェック

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by supporting TLS Certificate Status Request extension [RFC6066] during tunnel establishment.

TEAPv1 は、トンネルの確立の間に TLS Certificate Status Request 拡張 [RFC6066] をサポートすることで、この要件を満たしています。

A.22. Requirement 4.5.2: Internationalization

A.22. 要件 4.5.2: 国際化

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by supporting UTF-8 format in Basic-Password-Auth-Req TLV as defined in Section 4.2.14 and Basic-Password-Auth-Resp TLV as defined in Section 4.2.15.

TEAPv1 は、Section 4.2.14 で定義されている Basic-Password-Auth-Req TLV と Section 4.2.15 で定義されている Basic-Password-Auth-Resp TLV で UTF-8 形式をサポートすることで、この要件を満たしています。

A.23. Requirement 4.5.3: Metadata

A.23. 要件 4.5.3: メタデータ

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by supporting Identity-Type TLV as defined in Section 4.2.3 to indicate whether the authentication is for a user or a machine.

TEAPv1 は、認証がユーザーのためのものかマシンのためのものかを示すために、Section 4.2.3 で定義されている Identity-Type TLV をサポートすることで、この要件を満たしています。

A.24. Requirement 4.5.4: Password Change

A.24. 要件 4.5.4: パスワードの変更

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by supporting multiple Basic-Password-Auth-Req TLV and Basic-Password-Auth-Resp TLV exchanges within a single EAP authentication, which allows "housekeeping"" functions such as password change.

TEAPv1 は、単一の EAP 認証内で複数の Basic-Password-Auth-Req TLV と Basic-Password-Auth-Resp TLV のやり取りをサポートすることで、この要件を満たしています。これにより、パスワードの変更などの「管理上の」機能が可能になります。

A.25. Requirement 4.6.1: Method Negotiation

A.25. 要件 4.6.1: 方式の交渉

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by supporting inner EAP method negotiation within the protected TLS tunnel.

TEAPv1 は、保護された TLS トンネル内での内部の EAP 方式の交渉をサポートすることで、この要件を満たしています。

A.26. Requirement 4.6.2: Chained Methods

A.26. 要件 4.6.2: 連鎖した方式

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by supporting inner EAP method chaining within protected TLS tunnels as defined in Section 3.6.2.

TEAPv1 は、Section 3.6.2 で定義されているように、保護された TLS トンネル内での内部の EAP 方式の連鎖をサポートすることで、この要件を満たしています。

A.27. Requirement 4.6.3: Cryptographic Binding with the TLS Tunnel

A.27. 要件 4.6.3: TLS トンネルとの暗号的バインディング

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by supporting cryptographic binding of the inner EAP method keys with the keys derived from the TLS tunnel as defined in Section 4.2.13.

TEAPv1 は、Section 4.2.13 で定義されているように、内部の EAP 方式の鍵と TLS トンネルから導出された鍵との暗号的バインディングをサポートすることで、この要件を満たしています。

A.28. Requirement 4.6.4: Peer-Initiated EAP Authentication

A.28. 要件 4.6.4: ピアが開始する EAP 認証

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by supporting the Request-Action TLV as defined in Section 4.2.9 to allow a peer to initiate another inner EAP method.

TEAPv1 は、ピアが別の内部の EAP 方式を開始できるように、Section 4.2.9 で定義されている Request-Action TLV をサポートすることで、この要件を満たしています。

A.29. Requirement 4.6.5: Method Metadata

A.29. 要件 4.6.5: 方式のメタデータ

TEAPv1 meets this requirement by supporting the Identity-Type TLV as defined in Section 4.2.3 to indicate whether the authentication is for a user or a machine.

TEAPv1 は、認証がユーザーのためのものかマシンのためのものかを示すために、Section 4.2.3 で定義されている Identity-Type TLV をサポートすることで、この要件を満たしています。

Appendix B. Major Differences from EAP-FAST

Appendix B. EAP-FAST からの主な違い

This document is a new standard tunnel EAP method based on revision of EAP-FAST version 1 [RFC4851] that contains improved flexibility, particularly for negotiation of cryptographic algorithms. The major changes are:

この文書は、特に暗号アルゴリズムの交渉について柔軟性を向上させた、EAP-FAST バージョン 1 [RFC4851] の改訂に基づく新しい標準のトンネル EAP 方式です。主な変更は次のとおりです。

  1. The EAP method name has been changed from EAP-FAST to TEAP; this change thus requires that a new EAP Type be assigned.

  2. This version of TEAP MUST support TLS 1.2 [RFC5246]. TLS 1.1 and earlier MUST NOT be used with TEAP.

  3. The key derivation now makes use of TLS keying material exporters [RFC5705] and the PRF and hash function negotiated in TLS. This is to simplify implementation and better support cryptographic algorithm agility.

  4. TEAP is in full conformance with the SessionTicket extension [RFC5077].

  5. Support is provided for passing optional Outer TLVs in the first two message exchanges, in addition to the Authority-ID TLV data in EAP-FAST.

  6. Basic password authentication on the TLV level has been added in addition to the existing inner EAP method.

  7. Additional TLV types have been defined to support EAP channel binding and metadata. They are the Identity-Type TLV and Channel-Binding TLVs, defined in Section 4.2.

  1. EAP 方式の名前が EAP-FAST から TEAP に変更されました。そのため、この変更により新しい EAP Type の割り当てが必要になります。

  2. このバージョンの TEAP は TLS 1.2 [RFC5246] をサポートしなければなりません（MUST）。TLS 1.1 以前を TEAP で使用してはなりません（MUST NOT）。

  3. 鍵の導出では、TLS の鍵素材エクスポーター [RFC5705] と、TLS で交渉された PRF とハッシュ関数を利用するようになりました。これは、実装を簡単にし、暗号アルゴリズムの俊敏性をより良くサポートするためです。

  4. TEAP は SessionTicket 拡張 [RFC5077] に完全に準拠しています。

  5. EAP-FAST における Authority-ID TLV のデータに加えて、最初の 2 つのメッセージのやり取りでオプションの外部 TLV を渡すためのサポートが提供されます。

  6. 既存の内部の EAP 方式に加えて、TLV レベルでの基本的なパスワード認証が追加されました。

  7. EAP のチャネルバインディングとメタデータをサポートするために、追加の TLV タイプが定義されました。それらは Section 4.2 で定義されている Identity-Type TLV と Channel-Binding TLV です。

Appendix C. Examples

Appendix C. 例

C.1. Successful Authentication

C.1. 認証の成功

The following exchanges show a successful TEAP authentication with basic password authentication. The conversation will appear as follows:

次のやり取りは、基本的なパスワード認証による TEAP の認証の成功を示しています。対話は次のようになります。

   Authenticating Peer     Authenticator
   -------------------     -------------
                           <- EAP-Request/
                           Identity
   EAP-Response/
   Identity (MyID1) ->
                           <- EAP-Request/
                           EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                           (TEAP Start, S bit set, Authority-ID)

   EAP-Response/
   EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
   (TLS client_hello) ->

                           <- EAP-Request/
                           EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                           (TLS server_hello,
                           (TLS change_cipher_spec,
                            TLS finished)

   EAP-Response/
   EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1 ->
   (TLS change_cipher_spec,
    TLS finished)

   TLS channel established
   (messages sent within the TLS channel)

                          <- Basic-Password-Auth-Req TLV, Challenge

   Basic-Password-Auth-Resp TLV, Response with both
   username and password) ->

   optional additional exchanges (new pin mode,
   password change, etc.) ...

                        <- Intermediate-Result TLV (Success),
                            Crypto-Binding TLV (Request),
                            Result TLV (Success)

   Intermediate-Result TLV (Success),
   Crypto-Binding TLV(Response),
   Result TLV (Success) ->

   TLS channel torn down
   (messages sent in cleartext)

                           <- EAP-Success
   Authenticating Peer     Authenticator
   -------------------     -------------
                           <- EAP-Request/
                           Identity
   EAP-Response/
   Identity (MyID1) ->
                           <- EAP-Request/
                           EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                           (TEAP Start, S bit set, Authority-ID)

   EAP-Response/
   EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
   (TLS client_hello) ->

                           <- EAP-Request/
                           EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                           (TLS server_hello,
                           (TLS change_cipher_spec,
                            TLS finished)

   EAP-Response/
   EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1 ->
   (TLS change_cipher_spec,
    TLS finished)

   TLS channel established
   (messages sent within the TLS channel)

                          <- Basic-Password-Auth-Req TLV, Challenge

   Basic-Password-Auth-Resp TLV, Response with both
   username and password) ->

   optional additional exchanges (new pin mode,
   password change, etc.) ...

                        <- Intermediate-Result TLV (Success),
                            Crypto-Binding TLV (Request),
                            Result TLV (Success)

   Intermediate-Result TLV (Success),
   Crypto-Binding TLV(Response),
   Result TLV (Success) ->

   TLS channel torn down
   (messages sent in cleartext)

                           <- EAP-Success

C.2. Failed Authentication

C.2. 認証の失敗

The following exchanges show a failed TEAP authentication due to wrong user credentials. The conversation will appear as follows:

次のやり取りは、ユーザーの資格情報が誤っているために失敗した TEAP の認証を示しています。対話は次のようになります。

   Authenticating Peer     Authenticator
   -------------------     -------------
                           <- EAP-Request/Identity

   EAP-Response/
   Identity (MyID1) ->


                           <- EAP-Request/
                           EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                           (TEAP Start, S bit set, Authority-ID)

   EAP-Response/
   EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
   (TLS client_hello) ->

                           <- EAP-Request/
                           EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                           (TLS server_hello,
                           (TLS change_cipher_spec,
                            TLS finished)

   EAP-Response/
   EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1 ->
   (TLS change_cipher_spec,
    TLS finished)

   TLS channel established
   (messages sent within the TLS channel)

                          <- Basic-Password-Auth-Req TLV, Challenge

   Basic-Password-Auth-Resp TLV, Response with both
   username and password) ->

                           <- Intermediate-Result TLV (Failure),
                              Result TLV (Failure)

   Intermediate-Result TLV (Failure),
   Result TLV (Failure) ->

   TLS channel torn down
   (messages sent in cleartext)

                           <- EAP-Failure
   Authenticating Peer     Authenticator
   -------------------     -------------
                           <- EAP-Request/Identity

   EAP-Response/
   Identity (MyID1) ->


                           <- EAP-Request/
                           EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                           (TEAP Start, S bit set, Authority-ID)

   EAP-Response/
   EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
   (TLS client_hello) ->

                           <- EAP-Request/
                           EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                           (TLS server_hello,
                           (TLS change_cipher_spec,
                            TLS finished)

   EAP-Response/
   EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1 ->
   (TLS change_cipher_spec,
    TLS finished)

   TLS channel established
   (messages sent within the TLS channel)

                          <- Basic-Password-Auth-Req TLV, Challenge

   Basic-Password-Auth-Resp TLV, Response with both
   username and password) ->

                           <- Intermediate-Result TLV (Failure),
                              Result TLV (Failure)

   Intermediate-Result TLV (Failure),
   Result TLV (Failure) ->

   TLS channel torn down
   (messages sent in cleartext)

                           <- EAP-Failure

C.3. Full TLS Handshake Using Certificate-Based Cipher Suite

C.3. 証明書ベースの暗号スイートを使用した完全な TLS ハンドシェイク

In the case within TEAP Phase 1 where an abbreviated TLS handshake is tried, fails, and falls back to the certificate-based full TLS handshake, the conversation will appear as follows:

TEAP のフェーズ 1 内で、簡略化された TLS ハンドシェイクが試みられて失敗し、証明書ベースの完全な TLS ハンドシェイクにフォールバックする場合、対話は次のようになります。

  Authenticating Peer    Authenticator
  -------------------    -------------
                         <- EAP-Request/Identity
  EAP-Response/
  Identity (MyID1) ->

  // Identity sent in the clear.  May be a hint to help route
     the authentication request to EAP server, instead of the
     full user identity.

                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TEAP Start, S bit set, Authority-ID)
  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  (TLS client_hello with
  SessionTicket extension)->

  // If the server rejects the session resumption,
     it falls through to the full TLS handshake.

                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS server_hello,
                           TLS certificate,
                          [TLS server_key_exchange,]
                          [TLS certificate_request,]
                           TLS server_hello_done)

  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  ([TLS certificate,]
   TLS client_key_exchange,
  [TLS certificate_verify,]
   TLS change_cipher_spec,
   TLS finished) ->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS change_cipher_spec,
                           TLS finished,
                           EAP-Payload TLV[EAP-Request/
                           Identity])

  // TLS channel established
     (messages sent within the TLS channel)

  // First EAP Payload TLV is coalesced with the TLS Finished as
     Application Data and protected by the TLS tunnel.

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/Identity (MyID2)]->

  // identity protected by TLS.

                           <- EAP-Payload TLV
                           [EAP-Request/EAP-Type=X]

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/EAP-Type=X] ->

  // Method X exchanges followed by Protected Termination

                       <- Intermediate-Result TLV (Success),
                           Crypto-Binding TLV (Request),
                           Result TLV (Success)

  Intermediate-Result TLV (Success),
  Crypto-Binding TLV (Response),
  Result TLV (Success) ->

  // TLS channel torn down
  (messages sent in cleartext)

                          <- EAP-Success
  Authenticating Peer    Authenticator
  -------------------    -------------
                         <- EAP-Request/Identity
  EAP-Response/
  Identity (MyID1) ->

  // Identity sent in the clear.  May be a hint to help route
     the authentication request to EAP server, instead of the
     full user identity.

                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TEAP Start, S bit set, Authority-ID)
  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  (TLS client_hello with
  SessionTicket extension)->

  // If the server rejects the session resumption,
     it falls through to the full TLS handshake.

                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS server_hello,
                           TLS certificate,
                          [TLS server_key_exchange,]
                          [TLS certificate_request,]
                           TLS server_hello_done)

  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  ([TLS certificate,]
   TLS client_key_exchange,
  [TLS certificate_verify,]
   TLS change_cipher_spec,
   TLS finished) ->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS change_cipher_spec,
                           TLS finished,
                           EAP-Payload TLV[EAP-Request/
                           Identity])

  // TLS channel established
     (messages sent within the TLS channel)

  // First EAP Payload TLV is coalesced with the TLS Finished as
     Application Data and protected by the TLS tunnel.

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/Identity (MyID2)]->

  // identity protected by TLS.

                           <- EAP-Payload TLV
                           [EAP-Request/EAP-Type=X]

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/EAP-Type=X] ->

  // Method X exchanges followed by Protected Termination

                       <- Intermediate-Result TLV (Success),
                           Crypto-Binding TLV (Request),
                           Result TLV (Success)

  Intermediate-Result TLV (Success),
  Crypto-Binding TLV (Response),
  Result TLV (Success) ->

  // TLS channel torn down
  (messages sent in cleartext)

                          <- EAP-Success

C.4. Client Authentication During Phase 1 with Identity Privacy

C.4. アイデンティティーのプライバシーを伴うフェーズ 1 でのクライアント認証

In the case where a certificate-based TLS handshake occurs within TEAP Phase 1 and client certificate authentication and identity privacy is desired (and therefore TLS renegotiation is being used to transmit the peer credentials in the protected TLS tunnel), the conversation will appear as follows for TLS 1.2:

TEAP のフェーズ 1 内で証明書ベースの TLS ハンドシェイクが行われ、クライアント証明書の認証とアイデンティティーのプライバシーが望まれる（そのため、保護された TLS トンネルでピアの資格情報を送信するために TLS の再交渉が使用される）場合、TLS 1.2 では対話は次のようになります。

  Authenticating Peer     Authenticator
  -------------------     -------------
                         <- EAP-Request/Identity
  EAP-Response/
  Identity (MyID1) ->

  // Identity sent in the clear.  May be a hint to help route
     the authentication request to EAP server, instead of the
     full user identity.

                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TEAP Start, S bit set, Authority-ID)
  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  (TLS client_hello)->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS server_hello,
                           TLS certificate,
                          [TLS server_key_exchange,]
                          [TLS certificate_request,]
                           TLS server_hello_done)
  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  (TLS client_key_exchange,
   TLS change_cipher_spec,
   TLS finished) ->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS change_cipher_spec,
                           TLS finished,
                           EAP-Payload TLV[EAP-Request/
                           Identity])

  // TLS channel established
     (EAP Payload messages sent within the TLS channel)

  // peer sends TLS client_hello to request TLS renegotiation
  TLS client_hello ->

                          <- TLS server_hello,
                           TLS certificate,
                           [TLS server_key_exchange,]
                           [TLS certificate_request,]
                           TLS server_hello_done
  [TLS certificate,]
   TLS client_key_exchange,
  [TLS certificate_verify,]
   TLS change_cipher_spec,
   TLS finished ->

                          <- TLS change_cipher_spec,
                             TLS finished,
                             Crypto-Binding TLV (Request),
                             Result TLV (Success)

  Crypto-Binding TLV (Response),
  Result TLV (Success)) ->

  //TLS channel torn down
  (messages sent in cleartext)

                          <- EAP-Success
  Authenticating Peer     Authenticator
  -------------------     -------------
                         <- EAP-Request/Identity
  EAP-Response/
  Identity (MyID1) ->

  // Identity sent in the clear.  May be a hint to help route
     the authentication request to EAP server, instead of the
     full user identity.

                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TEAP Start, S bit set, Authority-ID)
  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  (TLS client_hello)->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS server_hello,
                           TLS certificate,
                          [TLS server_key_exchange,]
                          [TLS certificate_request,]
                           TLS server_hello_done)
  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  (TLS client_key_exchange,
   TLS change_cipher_spec,
   TLS finished) ->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS change_cipher_spec,
                           TLS finished,
                           EAP-Payload TLV[EAP-Request/
                           Identity])

  // TLS channel established
     (EAP Payload messages sent within the TLS channel)

  // peer sends TLS client_hello to request TLS renegotiation
  TLS client_hello ->

                          <- TLS server_hello,
                           TLS certificate,
                           [TLS server_key_exchange,]
                           [TLS certificate_request,]
                           TLS server_hello_done
  [TLS certificate,]
   TLS client_key_exchange,
  [TLS certificate_verify,]
   TLS change_cipher_spec,
   TLS finished ->

                          <- TLS change_cipher_spec,
                             TLS finished,
                             Crypto-Binding TLV (Request),
                             Result TLV (Success)

  Crypto-Binding TLV (Response),
  Result TLV (Success)) ->

  //TLS channel torn down
  (messages sent in cleartext)

                          <- EAP-Success

C.5. Fragmentation and Reassembly

C.5. 断片化と再組み立て

In the case where TEAP fragmentation is required, the conversation will appear as follows:

TEAP の断片化が必要な場合、対話は次のようになります。

  Authenticating Peer     Authenticator
  -------------------     -------------
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          Identity
  EAP-Response/
  Identity (MyID) ->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TEAP Start, S bit set, Authority-ID)

  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  (TLS client_hello)->

                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS server_hello,
                           TLS certificate,
                          [TLS server_key_exchange,]
                          [TLS certificate_request,]
                           TLS server_hello_done)
                          (Fragment 1: L, M bits set)

  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1 ->

                          <- EAP-Request/
                             EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (Fragment 2: M bit set)
  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1 ->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (Fragment 3)
  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  ([TLS certificate,]
   TLS client_key_exchange,
  [TLS certificate_verify,]
   TLS change_cipher_spec,
   TLS finished)
   (Fragment 1: L, M bits set)->

                           <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  (Fragment 2)->
                         <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS change_cipher_spec,
                           TLS finished,
                          [EAP-Payload TLV[
                          EAP-Request/Identity]])

  // TLS channel established
     (messages sent within the TLS channel)

  // First EAP Payload TLV is coalesced with the TLS Finished as
     Application Data and protected by the TLS tunnel.

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/Identity (MyID2)]->

  // identity protected by TLS.

                           <- EAP-Payload TLV
                           [EAP-Request/EAP-Type=X]

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/EAP-Type=X] ->

  // Method X exchanges followed by Protected Termination

                       <- Intermediate-Result TLV (Success),
                           Crypto-Binding TLV (Request),
                           Result TLV (Success)

  Intermediate-Result TLV (Success),
  Crypto-Binding TLV (Response),
  Result TLV (Success) ->

  // TLS channel torn down
  (messages sent in cleartext)

                          <- EAP-Success
  Authenticating Peer     Authenticator
  -------------------     -------------
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          Identity
  EAP-Response/
  Identity (MyID) ->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TEAP Start, S bit set, Authority-ID)

  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  (TLS client_hello)->

                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS server_hello,
                           TLS certificate,
                          [TLS server_key_exchange,]
                          [TLS certificate_request,]
                           TLS server_hello_done)
                          (Fragment 1: L, M bits set)

  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1 ->

                          <- EAP-Request/
                             EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (Fragment 2: M bit set)
  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1 ->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (Fragment 3)
  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  ([TLS certificate,]
   TLS client_key_exchange,
  [TLS certificate_verify,]
   TLS change_cipher_spec,
   TLS finished)
   (Fragment 1: L, M bits set)->

                           <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  (Fragment 2)->
                         <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS change_cipher_spec,
                           TLS finished,
                          [EAP-Payload TLV[
                          EAP-Request/Identity]])

  // TLS channel established
     (messages sent within the TLS channel)

  // First EAP Payload TLV is coalesced with the TLS Finished as
     Application Data and protected by the TLS tunnel.

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/Identity (MyID2)]->

  // identity protected by TLS.

                           <- EAP-Payload TLV
                           [EAP-Request/EAP-Type=X]

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/EAP-Type=X] ->

  // Method X exchanges followed by Protected Termination

                       <- Intermediate-Result TLV (Success),
                           Crypto-Binding TLV (Request),
                           Result TLV (Success)

  Intermediate-Result TLV (Success),
  Crypto-Binding TLV (Response),
  Result TLV (Success) ->

  // TLS channel torn down
  (messages sent in cleartext)

                          <- EAP-Success

C.6. Sequence of EAP Methods

C.6. EAP 方式の並び

When TEAP is negotiated with a sequence of EAP method X followed by method Y, the conversation will occur as follows:

EAP 方式 X とそれに続く方式 Y の並びで TEAP が交渉される場合、対話は次のように行われます。

  Authenticating Peer     Authenticator
  -------------------     -------------
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          Identity
  EAP-Response/
  Identity (MyID1) ->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TEAP Start, S bit set, Authority-ID)

  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  (TLS client_hello)->

                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS server_hello,
                           TLS certificate,
                          [TLS server_key_exchange,]
                          [TLS certificate_request,]
                           TLS server_hello_done)
  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  ([TLS certificate,]
   TLS client_key_exchange,
  [TLS certificate_verify,]
   TLS change_cipher_spec,
   TLS finished) ->
                         <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS change_cipher_spec,
                           TLS finished,
                           Identity-Type TLV,
                          EAP-Payload TLV[
                          EAP-Request/Identity])

  // TLS channel established
     (messages sent within the TLS channel)

  // First EAP Payload TLV is coalesced with the TLS Finished as
     Application Data and protected by the TLS tunnel

  Identity_Type TLV
  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/Identity] ->

                          <- EAP-Payload TLV
                        [EAP-Request/EAP-Type=X]

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/EAP-Type=X] ->

         // Optional additional X Method exchanges...

                         <- EAP-Payload TLV
                        [EAP-Request/EAP-Type=X]

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/EAP-Type=X]->

                          <- Intermediate Result TLV (Success),
                           Crypto-Binding TLV (Request),
                           Identity-Type TLV,
                          EAP-Payload TLV[
                          EAP-Request/Identity])

  // Compound MAC calculated using keys generated from
     EAP method X and the TLS tunnel.

  // Next EAP conversation started (with EAP-Request/Identity)
     after successful completion of previous method X.  The
     Intermediate-Result and Crypto-Binding TLVs are sent in
     the next packet to minimize round trips.

  Intermediate Result TLV (Success),
  Crypto-Binding TLV (Response),
  EAP-Payload TLV [EAP-Response/Identity (MyID2)] ->

         // Optional additional EAP method Y exchanges...

                         <- EAP Payload TLV [
                         EAP-Type=Y]

  EAP Payload TLV
  [EAP-Type=Y] ->

                         <- Intermediate-Result TLV (Success),
                           Crypto-Binding TLV (Request),
                           Result TLV (Success)

  Intermediate-Result TLV (Success),
  Crypto-Binding TLV (Response),
  Result TLV (Success) ->

  // Compound MAC calculated using keys generated from EAP
     methods X and Y and the TLS tunnel.  Compound keys are
     generated using keys generated from EAP methods X and Y
     and the TLS tunnel.

  // TLS channel torn down (messages sent in cleartext)

                          <- EAP-Success
  Authenticating Peer     Authenticator
  -------------------     -------------
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          Identity
  EAP-Response/
  Identity (MyID1) ->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TEAP Start, S bit set, Authority-ID)

  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  (TLS client_hello)->

                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS server_hello,
                           TLS certificate,
                          [TLS server_key_exchange,]
                          [TLS certificate_request,]
                           TLS server_hello_done)
  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  ([TLS certificate,]
   TLS client_key_exchange,
  [TLS certificate_verify,]
   TLS change_cipher_spec,
   TLS finished) ->
                         <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS change_cipher_spec,
                           TLS finished,
                           Identity-Type TLV,
                          EAP-Payload TLV[
                          EAP-Request/Identity])

  // TLS channel established
     (messages sent within the TLS channel)

  // First EAP Payload TLV is coalesced with the TLS Finished as
     Application Data and protected by the TLS tunnel

  Identity_Type TLV
  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/Identity] ->

                          <- EAP-Payload TLV
                        [EAP-Request/EAP-Type=X]

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/EAP-Type=X] ->

         // Optional additional X Method exchanges...

                         <- EAP-Payload TLV
                        [EAP-Request/EAP-Type=X]

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/EAP-Type=X]->

                          <- Intermediate Result TLV (Success),
                           Crypto-Binding TLV (Request),
                           Identity-Type TLV,
                          EAP-Payload TLV[
                          EAP-Request/Identity])

  // Compound MAC calculated using keys generated from
     EAP method X and the TLS tunnel.

  // Next EAP conversation started (with EAP-Request/Identity)
     after successful completion of previous method X.  The
     Intermediate-Result and Crypto-Binding TLVs are sent in
     the next packet to minimize round trips.

  Intermediate Result TLV (Success),
  Crypto-Binding TLV (Response),
  EAP-Payload TLV [EAP-Response/Identity (MyID2)] ->

         // Optional additional EAP method Y exchanges...

                         <- EAP Payload TLV [
                         EAP-Type=Y]

  EAP Payload TLV
  [EAP-Type=Y] ->

                         <- Intermediate-Result TLV (Success),
                           Crypto-Binding TLV (Request),
                           Result TLV (Success)

  Intermediate-Result TLV (Success),
  Crypto-Binding TLV (Response),
  Result TLV (Success) ->

  // Compound MAC calculated using keys generated from EAP
     methods X and Y and the TLS tunnel.  Compound keys are
     generated using keys generated from EAP methods X and Y
     and the TLS tunnel.

  // TLS channel torn down (messages sent in cleartext)

                          <- EAP-Success

C.7. Failed Crypto-Binding

C.7. 暗号的バインディングの失敗

The following exchanges show a failed crypto-binding validation. The conversation will appear as follows:

次のやり取りは、暗号的バインディングの検証の失敗を示しています。対話は次のようになります。

  Authenticating Peer     Authenticator
  -------------------     -------------
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          Identity
  EAP-Response/
  Identity (MyID1) ->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TEAP Start, S bit set, Authority-ID)

  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  (TLS client_hello) ->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS Server Key Exchange
                           TLS Server Hello Done)
  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1 ->
  (TLS Client Key Exchange
   TLS change_cipher_spec,
   TLS finished)

                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS change_cipher_spec
                           TLS finished)
                           EAP-Payload TLV[
                           EAP-Request/Identity])

     // TLS channel established
        (messages sent within the TLS channel)

     // First EAP Payload TLV is coalesced with the TLS Finished as
        Application Data and protected by the TLS tunnel.

  EAP-Payload TLV/
  EAP Identity Response ->

                         <-  EAP Payload TLV, EAP-Request,
                             (EAP-FAST-MSCHAPV2, Challenge)

  EAP Payload TLV, EAP-Response,
  (EAP-FAST-MSCHAPV2, Response) ->

                         <-  EAP Payload TLV, EAP-Request,
                             (EAP-FAST-MSCHAPV2, Success Request)

  EAP Payload TLV, EAP-Response,
  (EAP-FAST-MSCHAPV2, Success Response) ->

                       <- Intermediate-Result TLV (Success),
                           Crypto-Binding TLV (Request),
                              Result TLV (Success)

     Intermediate-Result TLV (Success),
     Result TLV (Failure)
     Error TLV with
     (Error Code = 2001) ->

  // TLS channel torn down
     (messages sent in cleartext)

                       <- EAP-Failure
  Authenticating Peer     Authenticator
  -------------------     -------------
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          Identity
  EAP-Response/
  Identity (MyID1) ->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TEAP Start, S bit set, Authority-ID)

  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  (TLS client_hello) ->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS Server Key Exchange
                           TLS Server Hello Done)
  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1 ->
  (TLS Client Key Exchange
   TLS change_cipher_spec,
   TLS finished)

                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS change_cipher_spec
                           TLS finished)
                           EAP-Payload TLV[
                           EAP-Request/Identity])

     // TLS channel established
        (messages sent within the TLS channel)

     // First EAP Payload TLV is coalesced with the TLS Finished as
        Application Data and protected by the TLS tunnel.

  EAP-Payload TLV/
  EAP Identity Response ->

                         <-  EAP Payload TLV, EAP-Request,
                             (EAP-FAST-MSCHAPV2, Challenge)

  EAP Payload TLV, EAP-Response,
  (EAP-FAST-MSCHAPV2, Response) ->

                         <-  EAP Payload TLV, EAP-Request,
                             (EAP-FAST-MSCHAPV2, Success Request)

  EAP Payload TLV, EAP-Response,
  (EAP-FAST-MSCHAPV2, Success Response) ->

                       <- Intermediate-Result TLV (Success),
                           Crypto-Binding TLV (Request),
                              Result TLV (Success)

     Intermediate-Result TLV (Success),
     Result TLV (Failure)
     Error TLV with
     (Error Code = 2001) ->

  // TLS channel torn down
     (messages sent in cleartext)

                       <- EAP-Failure

C.8. Sequence of EAP Method with Vendor-Specific TLV Exchange

C.8. Vendor-Specific TLV のやり取りを伴う EAP 方式の並び

When TEAP is negotiated with a sequence of EAP methods followed by a Vendor-Specific TLV exchange, the conversation will occur as follows:

EAP 方式の並びとそれに続く Vendor-Specific TLV のやり取りで TEAP が交渉される場合、対話は次のように行われます。

  Authenticating Peer     Authenticator
  -------------------     -------------
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          Identity
  EAP-Response/
  Identity (MyID1) ->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TEAP Start, S bit set, Authority-ID)

  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  (TLS client_hello)->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS server_hello,
                           TLS certificate,
                   [TLS server_key_exchange,]
                   [TLS certificate_request,]
                       TLS server_hello_done)

  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  ([TLS certificate,]
   TLS client_key_exchange,
  [TLS certificate_verify,]
   TLS change_cipher_spec,
   TLS finished) ->
                         <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS change_cipher_spec,
                           TLS finished,
                          EAP-Payload TLV[
                          EAP-Request/Identity])

  // TLS channel established
     (messages sent within the TLS channel)

  // First EAP Payload TLV is coalesced with the TLS Finished as
     Application Data and protected by the TLS tunnel.

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/Identity] ->

                        <- EAP-Payload TLV
                        [EAP-Request/EAP-Type=X]

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/EAP-Type=X] ->

                         <- EAP-Payload TLV
                        [EAP-Request/EAP-Type=X]

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/EAP-Type=X]->

                          <- Intermediate Result TLV (Success),
                           Crypto-Binding TLV (Request),
                           Vendor-Specific TLV,

  // Vendor-Specific TLV exchange started after successful
     completion of previous method X.  The Intermediate-Result
     and Crypto-Binding TLVs are sent with Vendor-Specific TLV
     in next packet to minimize round trips.

  // Compound MAC calculated using keys generated from
     EAP method X and the TLS tunnel.

  Intermediate Result TLV (Success),
  Crypto-Binding TLV (Response),
  Vendor-Specific TLV ->

      // Optional additional Vendor-Specific TLV exchanges...

                         <- Vendor-Specific TLV

  Vendor-Specific TLV ->
                         <- Result TLV (Success)

  Result TLV (Success) ->

  // TLS channel torn down (messages sent in cleartext)

                          <- EAP-Success
  Authenticating Peer     Authenticator
  -------------------     -------------
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          Identity
  EAP-Response/
  Identity (MyID1) ->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TEAP Start, S bit set, Authority-ID)

  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  (TLS client_hello)->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS server_hello,
                           TLS certificate,
                   [TLS server_key_exchange,]
                   [TLS certificate_request,]
                       TLS server_hello_done)

  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  ([TLS certificate,]
   TLS client_key_exchange,
  [TLS certificate_verify,]
   TLS change_cipher_spec,
   TLS finished) ->
                         <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS change_cipher_spec,
                           TLS finished,
                          EAP-Payload TLV[
                          EAP-Request/Identity])

  // TLS channel established
     (messages sent within the TLS channel)

  // First EAP Payload TLV is coalesced with the TLS Finished as
     Application Data and protected by the TLS tunnel.

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/Identity] ->

                        <- EAP-Payload TLV
                        [EAP-Request/EAP-Type=X]

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/EAP-Type=X] ->

                         <- EAP-Payload TLV
                        [EAP-Request/EAP-Type=X]

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/EAP-Type=X]->

                          <- Intermediate Result TLV (Success),
                           Crypto-Binding TLV (Request),
                           Vendor-Specific TLV,

  // Vendor-Specific TLV exchange started after successful
     completion of previous method X.  The Intermediate-Result
     and Crypto-Binding TLVs are sent with Vendor-Specific TLV
     in next packet to minimize round trips.

  // Compound MAC calculated using keys generated from
     EAP method X and the TLS tunnel.

  Intermediate Result TLV (Success),
  Crypto-Binding TLV (Response),
  Vendor-Specific TLV ->

      // Optional additional Vendor-Specific TLV exchanges...

                         <- Vendor-Specific TLV

  Vendor-Specific TLV ->
                         <- Result TLV (Success)

  Result TLV (Success) ->

  // TLS channel torn down (messages sent in cleartext)

                          <- EAP-Success

C.9. Peer Requests Inner Method After Server Sends Result TLV

C.9. サーバーが Result TLV を送信した後にピアが内部方式を要求する場合

In the case where the peer is authenticated during Phase 1 and the server sends back a Result TLV but the peer wants to request another Inner Method, the conversation will appear as follows:

ピアがフェーズ 1 の間に認証され、サーバーが Result TLV を返したものの、ピアが別の内部方式を要求したい場合、対話は次のようになります。

  Authenticating Peer    Authenticator
  -------------------    -------------
                         <- EAP-Request/Identity
  EAP-Response/
  Identity (MyID1) ->

  // Identity sent in the clear.  May be a hint to help route
     the authentication request to EAP server, instead of the
     full user identity. TLS client certificate is also sent.

                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TEAP Start, S bit set, Authority-ID)
  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  (TLS client_hello)->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS server_hello,
                           TLS certificate,
                          [TLS server_key_exchange,]
                          [TLS certificate_request,]
                           TLS server_hello_done)

  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  [TLS certificate,]
   TLS client_key_exchange,
  [TLS certificate_verify,]
   TLS change_cipher_spec,
   TLS finished ->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS change_cipher_spec,
                           TLS finished,
                           Crypto-Binding TLV (Request),
                            Result TLV (Success))

  // TLS channel established
     (TLV Payload messages sent within the TLS channel)

   Crypto-Binding TLV(Response),
   Request-Action TLV
   (Status=Failure, Action=Negotiate-EAP)->

                        <- EAP-Payload TLV
                            [EAP-Request/Identity]

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/Identity] ->

                        <- EAP-Payload TLV
                        [EAP-Request/EAP-Type=X]

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/EAP-Type=X] ->

                         <- EAP-Payload TLV
                        [EAP-Request/EAP-Type=X]

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/EAP-Type=X]->

                          <- Intermediate Result TLV (Success),
                             Crypto-Binding TLV (Request),
                             Result TLV (Success)

  Intermediate Result TLV (Success),
  Crypto-Binding TLV (Response),
  Result TLV (Success)) ->

  // TLS channel torn down
  (messages sent in cleartext)

                          <- EAP-Success
  Authenticating Peer    Authenticator
  -------------------    -------------
                         <- EAP-Request/Identity
  EAP-Response/
  Identity (MyID1) ->

  // Identity sent in the clear.  May be a hint to help route
     the authentication request to EAP server, instead of the
     full user identity. TLS client certificate is also sent.

                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TEAP Start, S bit set, Authority-ID)
  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  (TLS client_hello)->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS server_hello,
                           TLS certificate,
                          [TLS server_key_exchange,]
                          [TLS certificate_request,]
                           TLS server_hello_done)

  EAP-Response/
  EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
  [TLS certificate,]
   TLS client_key_exchange,
  [TLS certificate_verify,]
   TLS change_cipher_spec,
   TLS finished ->
                          <- EAP-Request/
                          EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                          (TLS change_cipher_spec,
                           TLS finished,
                           Crypto-Binding TLV (Request),
                            Result TLV (Success))

  // TLS channel established
     (TLV Payload messages sent within the TLS channel)

   Crypto-Binding TLV(Response),
   Request-Action TLV
   (Status=Failure, Action=Negotiate-EAP)->

                        <- EAP-Payload TLV
                            [EAP-Request/Identity]

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/Identity] ->

                        <- EAP-Payload TLV
                        [EAP-Request/EAP-Type=X]

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/EAP-Type=X] ->

                         <- EAP-Payload TLV
                        [EAP-Request/EAP-Type=X]

  EAP-Payload TLV
  [EAP-Response/EAP-Type=X]->

                          <- Intermediate Result TLV (Success),
                             Crypto-Binding TLV (Request),
                             Result TLV (Success)

  Intermediate Result TLV (Success),
  Crypto-Binding TLV (Response),
  Result TLV (Success)) ->

  // TLS channel torn down
  (messages sent in cleartext)

                          <- EAP-Success

C.10. Channel Binding

C.10. チャネルバインディング

The following exchanges show a successful TEAP authentication with basic password authentication and channel binding using a Request-Action TLV. The conversation will appear as follows:

次のやり取りは、基本的なパスワード認証と、Request-Action TLV を使用したチャネルバインディングによる TEAP の認証の成功を示しています。対話は次のようになります。

   Authenticating Peer     Authenticator
   -------------------     -------------
                           <- EAP-Request/
                           Identity
   EAP-Response/
   Identity (MyID1) ->

                           <- EAP-Request/
                           EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                           (TEAP Start, S bit set, Authority-ID)

   EAP-Response/
   EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
   (TLS client_hello) ->

                           <- EAP-Request/
                           EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                           (TLS server_hello,
                           (TLS change_cipher_spec,
                            TLS finished)

   EAP-Response/
   EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1 ->
   (TLS change_cipher_spec,
    TLS finished)

   TLS channel established
   (messages sent within the TLS channel)

                          <- Basic-Password-Auth-Req TLV, Challenge

   Basic-Password-Auth-Resp TLV, Response with both
   username and password) ->

   optional additional exchanges (new pin mode,
   password change, etc.) ...

                        <- Crypto-Binding TLV (Request),
                            Result TLV (Success),

   Crypto-Binding TLV(Response),
   Request-Action TLV
   (Status=Failure, Action=Process TLV,
   TLV=Channel-Binding TLV)->

                            <- Channel-Binding TLV (Response),
                            Result TLV (Success),

   Result TLV (Success) ->

   TLS channel torn down
   (messages sent in cleartext)

                           <- EAP-Success
   Authenticating Peer     Authenticator
   -------------------     -------------
                           <- EAP-Request/
                           Identity
   EAP-Response/
   Identity (MyID1) ->

                           <- EAP-Request/
                           EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                           (TEAP Start, S bit set, Authority-ID)

   EAP-Response/
   EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
   (TLS client_hello) ->

                           <- EAP-Request/
                           EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1
                           (TLS server_hello,
                           (TLS change_cipher_spec,
                            TLS finished)

   EAP-Response/
   EAP-Type=TEAP, V=1 ->
   (TLS change_cipher_spec,
    TLS finished)

   TLS channel established
   (messages sent within the TLS channel)

                          <- Basic-Password-Auth-Req TLV, Challenge

   Basic-Password-Auth-Resp TLV, Response with both
   username and password) ->

   optional additional exchanges (new pin mode,
   password change, etc.) ...

                        <- Crypto-Binding TLV (Request),
                            Result TLV (Success),

   Crypto-Binding TLV(Response),
   Request-Action TLV
   (Status=Failure, Action=Process TLV,
   TLV=Channel-Binding TLV)->

                            <- Channel-Binding TLV (Response),
                            Result TLV (Success),

   Result TLV (Success) ->

   TLS channel torn down
   (messages sent in cleartext)

                           <- EAP-Success

C.11. PKCS Exchange

C.11. PKCS のやり取り

The following exchanges show the peer sending a PKCS#10 TLV and server replying with a PKCS7 TLV. The exchange below assumes that the EAP peer is authenticated in Phase 1, either via bidirectional certificate exchange or some other TLS method such as a proof of knowledge (TLS-POK). The conversation will appear as follows:

次のやり取りは、ピアが PKCS#10 TLV を送信し、サーバーが PKCS7 TLV で応答することを示しています。以下のやり取りは、双方向の証明書のやり取りか、知識の証明（TLS-POK）などの他の TLS の方法のいずれかによって、EAP ピアがフェーズ 1 で認証されていることを前提としています。対話は次のようになります。

,----.                                             ,-------.
|Peer|                                             |AuthSrv|
`-+--'                                             `---+---'
  |               EAP-Request / Identity               |
  | <- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
  |                                                    |
  |           EAP-Response / Identity (MYID1)          |
  |  - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - >
  |                                                    |
  |             EAP-Request/EAP-Type=TEAP,             |
  |              V=1(TEAP Start,                       |
  |              S bit set,                            |
  |              Authority-ID)                         |
  | <- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
  |                                                    |
  |             EAP-Response/EAP-Type=TEAP,            |
  |              V=1(TLS client_hello)                 |
  |  - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - >
  |                                                    |
  |             EAP-Request/ EAP-Type=TEAP,            |
  |              V=1(TLS server_hello,                 |
  |              TLS certificate,                      |
  |              TLS certificate_request,              |
  |              TLS finished)                         |
  | <- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
  |                                                    |
  |        EAP-Response/EAP-Type=TEAP,                 |
  |         V=1(TLS change_cipher_spec,                |
  |             TLS certificate,                       |
  |        TLS finished) TLS channel established       |
  |  - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - >
  |                                                    |
  |               Send Request-Action TLV              |
  | <- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
  |                                                    |
  |                   Send PKCS10 TLV                  |
  |  - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - >
  |                                                    |
  | Sign the CSR and send PKCS7 TLV Intermediate-Result|
  | TLV request(Success),                              |
  |  Crypto-Binding TLV(Request),                      |
  |  Result TLV(Success)                               |
  | <- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
  |                                                    |
  |     Intermediate-Result TLV response(Success),     |
  |      Crypto-Binding TLV(Response),                 |
  |      Result TLV(Success)                           |
  |  - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - >
  |                                                    |
  |                     EAP Success                    |
  | <- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
,----.                                             ,-------.
|Peer|                                             |AuthSrv|
`-+--'                                             `---+---'
  |               EAP-Request / Identity               |
  | <- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
  |                                                    |
  |           EAP-Response / Identity (MYID1)          |
  |  - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - >
  |                                                    |
  |             EAP-Request/EAP-Type=TEAP,             |
  |              V=1(TEAP Start,                       |
  |              S bit set,                            |
  |              Authority-ID)                         |
  | <- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
  |                                                    |
  |             EAP-Response/EAP-Type=TEAP,            |
  |              V=1(TLS client_hello)                 |
  |  - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - >
  |                                                    |
  |             EAP-Request/ EAP-Type=TEAP,            |
  |              V=1(TLS server_hello,                 |
  |              TLS certificate,                      |
  |              TLS certificate_request,              |
  |              TLS finished)                         |
  | <- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -
  |                                                    |
  |        EAP-Response/EAP-Type=TEAP,                 |
  |         V=1(TLS change_cipher_spec,                |
  |             TLS certificate,                       |
  |        TLS finished) TLS channel established       |
  |  - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - >

  | <- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - -

Appendix D. Changes from RFC 7170

Appendix D. RFC 7170 からの変更点

Alan DeKok was added as an editor.

Alan DeKok が編集者として追加されました。

The document was converted to Markdown from the [RFC7170] text output.

文書は [RFC7170] のテキスト出力から Markdown に変換されました。

Any formatting changes from [RFC7170] may have resulted from changing from XML to Markdown as the source file when editing the draft.

[RFC7170] からの書式の変更は、ドラフトを編集する際のソースファイルを XML から Markdown に変更したことによって生じた可能性があります。

The IANA Considerations section was replaced with a note to change the IANA registry references to this document.

IANA に関する考慮事項の節は、IANA レジストリーの参照をこの文書に変更する旨の注記に置き換えられました。

A new section was added to explain that the inner EAP-MSCHAPv2 derivation follows EAP-FAST. This is the largest technical change from the previous revision of this document and follows existing implementations.

内部の EAP-MSCHAPv2 の導出が EAP-FAST に従うことを説明する新しい節が追加されました。これは、この文書の以前の改訂からの最大の技術的な変更であり、既存の実装に従ったものです。

Many small changes have been made throughout the document to correct inconsistencies and to address mistakes. At a high level:

不整合を修正し誤りに対処するために、文書全体にわたって多くの小さな変更が加えられました。高いレベルでは次のとおりです。

  • All open errata have been addressed.

  • A new term "Inner Method" has been defined.

  • The definitions and derivation of IMSK, S-IMCK, etc. have been corrected and clarified.

  • The diagrams in Appendix C have been updated to match the TEAP state machine.

  • 未解決の正誤表はすべて対処されました。

  • 新しい用語「内部方式（Inner Method）」が定義されました。

  • IMSK、S-IMCK などの定義と導出が修正され、明確化されました。

  • Appendix C の図が TEAP の状態機械に合うように更新されました。

All uses of the PAC were removed. It had not been implemented, and there were no plans by implementors to use it.

PAC の使用はすべて削除されました。PAC は実装されておらず、実装者にそれを使用する計画もありませんでした。

Text was added on recommendations for inner and outer identities.

内部と外部のアイデンティティーについての推奨事項に関する文が追加されました。

Section 6.2.5 was added late in the document life cycle in order to document accidental behavior that could result in interoperability issues.

相互運用性の問題につながる可能性のある偶然の振る舞いを文書化するため、文書のライフサイクルの後期に Section 6.2.5 が追加されました。

Acknowledgments

謝辞

Nearly all of the text in this document was taken directly from [RFC7170]. We are grateful to the original authors and reviewers for that document. The acknowledgments given here are only for the changes that resulted in this document.

この文書のほぼすべての文は [RFC7170] から直接取られています。その文書の元の著者とレビュアーに感謝します。ここでの謝辞は、この文書をもたらした変更についてのものだけです。

Alexander Clouter provided substantial and detailed technical feedback on nearly every aspect of the specification. The corrections in this document are based on his work.

Alexander Clouter は、仕様のほぼすべての側面について、実質的で詳細な技術的フィードバックを提供しました。この文書の修正はその作業に基づいています。

We wish to thank the many reviewers and commenters in the EMU WG, including Eliot Lear, Joe Salowey, Heikki Vatiainen, Bruno Pereria Vidal, and Michael Richardson. Many corner cases and edge conditions were caught and corrected as a result of their feedback.

Eliot Lear、Joe Salowey、Heikki Vatiainen、Bruno Pereria Vidal、Michael Richardson をはじめとする、EMU WG の多くのレビュアーとコメント提供者に感謝します。彼らのフィードバックの結果、多くの特殊なケースと境界条件が発見され、修正されました。

Jouni Malinin initially pointed out the issues with [RFC7170]. Those comments resulted in substantial discussion on the EMU WG mailing list, and eventually this document. Jouni also made substantial contributions in analyzing corner cases, which resulted in the text in Section 6.2.5.

Jouni Malinin は、[RFC7170] の問題を最初に指摘しました。そのコメントにより、EMU WG のメーリングリストで実質的な議論が行われ、最終的にこの文書が生まれました。Jouni は特殊なケースの分析にも大きく貢献し、その結果が Section 6.2.5 の文になりました。
